The same pages appear in the in-game Manual under Creators -> API reference. [page-id] names a Manual page.

=== API reference [api-reference]
Generated reference for documented modules and the game-api declarations they use.

This reference is generated from module manifests, Creators guides and the public @space-sim/game-api declarations. Each module page lists the facts its manifest declares; each game-api page shows the exact declarations with their notes. Modules appear here once their Creators guide follows the module guide template.
Spacelab package 0.6.4-alpha.1 · API space-sim-game-api/2 · manifest space-sim-module/2 · source digest 0817caf64a51d088
Three versions are independent: a module version (semver, such as 0.1.0), the API identifier a module is written against (such as space-sim-game-api/2) and a file-format identifier (such as space-sim-flight-plans/1). The v2 API is pre-freeze and can still change.

=== Independent follow cameras reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.camera-follow]
Reusable basic camera control with an explicit flight adapter and optional smart-camera policy.
- ID: spacelab.camera-follow
- Version: 0.3.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- view.camera@1.0.0 · VIEW_CAMERAS: ServiceKey<LocalCameraFactory>
- view.flight-camera@1.0.0 · FLIGHT_CAMERAS: ServiceKey<FlightCameraFactory>

## Requires
- view.smart-camera@1.0.0 (optional) · SMART_CAMERAS: ServiceKey<SmartCameraFactory>

## Composition
- Slots: view.camera, view.flight-camera
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- initialZoom: number, default 10, from 0.1 to 50. Initial camera zoom: Starting magnification for each independent view. Changing a view later affects that view only.
- referenceMetres: number, default 1500, from 1 to 1000000000. View span at zoom 1 (m): World metres framed by the shorter viewport dimension at zoom 1. Changes presentation scale only.
- orientationSeconds: number, default 0.12, from 0 to 2, step 0.01. Orientation smoothing (s): Wall seconds for camera rotation to approach the target orientation. Zero snaps immediately; simulation time is unchanged.

## Game API used
Camera, CameraExtension, CameraFocusTarget, CameraTarget, FLIGHT_CAMERAS, FlightCameraController, FlightCameraFactory, LocalCameraFactory, LocalCameraInput, LocalViewCamera, ModuleInitializationV2, SMART_CAMERAS, ServiceKey, SmartCameraControl, SmartCameraFactory, SmartTargetMatch, VIEW_CAMERAS, Vec, blendAngle, dragCamera
-> game-api flight-view.ts [api-reference/game-api/flight-view]
-> game-api modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
-> game-api platform.ts [api-reference/game-api/platform]
-> game-api presentation.ts [api-reference/game-api/presentation]
-> game-api render-math.ts [api-reference/game-api/render-math]
-> game-api smart-camera.ts [api-reference/game-api/smart-camera]
-> game-api state.ts [api-reference/game-api/state]

## Guide and example
Example source: examples/modules/camera-follow (build with npm run module:build -- examples/modules/camera-follow/module.json out.spacemod).
-> Independent follow cameras: how to use, replace and remove it [spacelab.camera-follow/usage]

=== LAN discovery reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.discovery-lan]
Borrowed text transports and explicit greeting codecs; no world dependencies.
- ID: spacelab.discovery-lan
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: network
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- net.lan-discovery@1.0.0 · LAN_DISCOVERY: ServiceKey<LanDiscoveryFactory>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: net.lan-discovery
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- timeoutMs: number, default 800, from 1 to 60000, step 1. Discovery probe timeout (ms): Maximum wait for one candidate server to return a compatible greeting. A scan can explicitly override this default.
- concurrency: number, default 32, from 1 to 64, step 1. Concurrent discovery probes: Maximum number of candidate addresses probed together. Lower values reduce network load; higher values complete large scans sooner.
- maxGreetingLength: number, default 16384, from 1 to 65536, step 1. Greeting text limit: Maximum UTF-16 characters accepted in a discovery greeting. Larger session messages are handled by the transport, not discovery.

## Game API used
Disposable, FoundSession, LAN_DISCOVERY, LanDiscovery, LanDiscoveryFactory, LanDiscoveryOptions, LanScanOptions, ModuleInitializationV2, ServiceKey, TextConnection, TransportProvider
-> game-api modules.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules]
-> game-api modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
-> game-api platform.ts [api-reference/game-api/platform]
-> game-api session-discovery.ts [api-reference/game-api/session-discovery]

## Guide and example
Example source: examples/modules/discovery-lan (build with npm run module:build -- examples/modules/discovery-lan/module.json out.spacemod).

=== Independent fleet views reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.fleet-view]
Selected-provider coast queries, per-view culling/cache and borrowed world reads.
- ID: spacelab.fleet-view
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- view.fleet@1.0.0 · FLEET_VIEWS: ServiceKey<FleetViewFactory>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: view.fleet
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- marginPixels: number, default 40, from 40 to 256, step 1. Fleet viewport margin (px): Conservative extra CSS-pixel margin when culling off-screen fleet objects. At least the existing renderer margin; does not change world positions.
- stillPixels: number, default 0.25, from 0 to 1, step 0.05. Cached coast motion limit (px): Reuse a solved presentation position only while its maximum possible motion stays below this CSS-pixel limit. Zero solves every view update; authoritative positions remain exact.

## Game API used
Coast, FLEET_VIEWS, FleetViewFactory, FleetViewInput, FleetViewStats, FlightFleetView, ModuleInitializationV2, RenderShip, ServiceKey, Ship, State, Vec, WorldRead, interpolateState
-> game-api engine.ts [api-reference/game-api/engine]

-> game-api presentation.ts [api-reference/game-api/presentation]
-> game-api render-math.ts [api-reference/game-api/render-math]
-> game-api state.ts [api-reference/game-api/state]
-> game-api world.ts [api-reference/game-api/world]

=== Progressive flight prediction reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.flight-prediction]
Cancellable trajectory previews that a view can trace a little at a time without touching live flight.
- ID: spacelab.flight-prediction
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- view.prediction@1.0.0 · PREDICTIONS: ServiceKey<PredictionFactory>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: view.prediction
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- maxMs: number, default 3, from 0.1 to 16, step 0.1. Prediction work per view (ms): Wall-time ceiling for one tracing update. Guest view overrides can lower their budget; physics and prediction results are unchanged.
- traceSamplesPerSecond: number, default 1000, from 1 to 1000, step 1. Analytic tracing rate (samples/s): Rate at which a progressive coast preview earns sample work. Changes how quickly the complete path appears, not simulation precision.
- numericalSamplesPerSecond: number, default 12000, from 120 to 24000, step 1. Surface/numerical tracing rate (samples/s): Sample-work rate for terrain/contact or numerical previews, still bounded by the work-time ceiling and existing per-update cap.

## Game API used
Controls, FlightPrediction, GhostEnd, GhostSample, ModuleInitializationV2, PREDICTIONS, PredictionFactory, PredictionSummary, ResolvedBody, ServiceKey, SimulationContext, State, SurfaceProfile, WorldRead
-> game-api context.ts [api-reference/game-api/context]
-> game-api engine.ts [api-reference/game-api/engine]
-> game-api flight-view.ts [api-reference/game-api/flight-view]
-> game-api modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
-> game-api state.ts [api-reference/game-api/state]
-> game-api surface.ts [api-reference/game-api/surface]
-> game-api universe.ts [api-reference/game-api/universe]

## Requires
- input.local-pilots@^1.0.0 · LOCAL_PILOTS: ServiceKey<LocalPilotFactory>
- input.flight-profiles@^1.0.0 · FLIGHT_PROFILES: ServiceKey<FlightControllerProfiles>
- view.flight-camera@^1.0.0 · FLIGHT_CAMERAS: ServiceKey<FlightCameraFactory>
- view.prediction@^1.0.0 · PREDICTIONS: ServiceKey<PredictionFactory>
- view.fleet@^1.0.0 · FLEET_VIEWS: ServiceKey<FleetViewFactory>
- view.local@^1.0.0 · LOCAL_VIEWS: ServiceKey<LocalViewFactory>
- input.gamepad@^1.0.0 (optional) · GAMEPAD_INPUT: ServiceKey<GamepadInputFactory>

## Composition
- Slots: view.flight-split
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- joinButton: number, default 9, from 0 to 31, step 1. Controller join button: Standard gamepad button index that lets an unclaimed controller join local play. Default 9 is Start/Menu/Options; ownership rules still apply.
- guestPredictionMs: number, default 1.5, from 0.1 to 10, step 0.1. Guest prediction budget (ms): Wall-time prediction budget for each guest view. Higher budgets refine previews sooner; they do not advance or alter the shared world.
- wheelZoom: number, default 0.0015, from 0.0001 to 0.01, step 0.0001. Guest wheel zoom sensitivity: Exponential zoom factor per mouse-wheel unit in guest panes. Each guest camera remains independent.
- separateScreenRatio: number, default 0.55, from 0.1 to 1, step 0.01. Screen separation threshold: Split a guest into its own pane when its screen distance exceeds this fraction of the viewport’s smaller dimension.
- mergeScreenRatio: number, default 0.35, from 0.05 to 1, step 0.01. Screen merge threshold: A separated guest must fall below this screen-distance fraction before merging. Must not exceed the separation threshold.
- separateDistance: number, default 5000, from 1 to 1000000, step 1. World separation threshold (m): Split a guest when its body-relative distance from Player 1 exceeds this distance, even if the shared camera is zoomed far out.
- mergeDistance: number, default 3500, from 1 to 1000000, step 1. World merge threshold (m): A guest must be closer than this distance before merging. Must not exceed the world separation threshold.
- mergeDelayMs: number, default 600, from 0 to 10000, step 1. Merge stability delay (ms): Keep a guest inside both merge thresholds for this long before returning to the shared view. Prevents rapid split/merge flicker.

## Game API used
Controls, FLEET_VIEWS, FLIGHT_CAMERAS, FLIGHT_PROFILES, FLIGHT_SPLIT, FlightActionId, FlightCameraController, FlightFleetView, FlightGuestPilot, FlightLocalSession, FlightPrediction, FlightSplitFactory, FlightSplitScreen, FlightSplitScreenHost, FlightSplitServices, GAMEPAD_INPUT, LOCAL_PILOTS, LOCAL_VIEWS, LocalPlayersConfig, ModuleInitializationV2, PREDICTIONS, Renderer, RendererProvider, ResolvedBody, ServiceKey, SessionRead, ShaderPack, Ship, State, Vec
-> game-api flight-input.ts [api-reference/game-api/flight-input]

- flight.transfers@^1.0.0 (optional) · TRANSFERS: ServiceKey<TransferPlanner>

## Composition

## Settings
- previewMaxMs: number, default 1, from 0.1 to 8, step 0.1. Maneuver preview work (ms): Per-update wall-time budget for the independent planned-burn preview. Leaves live physics and the main coast preview unchanged.
- uiRefreshMs: number, default 100, from 16 to 1000, step 1. Maneuver text refresh (ms): Minimum wall time between editor status/summary refreshes. Commands and trajectory validity still run at their existing boundaries.
- alignmentWarp: number, default 4, from 1 to 4, step 1. Automatic alignment warp (×): Ship rotation speed during explicitly authorized automatic approaches. Choose 1 for real time, or 2–4 to align faster using the same accurate rotation physics. Engine burns run at 1×. Follow-clock plans never increase warp.

=== Navball HUD reference [api-reference/module/builtin.navball]
Optional attitude instrument using the shared HUD lifecycle.
- ID: builtin.navball
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- hud.navball@1.0.0 · NAVBALL_HUD: ServiceKey<HudInstrumentDefinition>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: hud.navball
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- frame: number, default 0, from 0 to 2, step 1. Velocity reference frame: 0 automatically selects the surface or orbital frame; 1 always uses the orbital frame; 2 always uses the rotating surface frame.
- orientation: number, default 0, from 0 to 1, step 1. Dial orientation: 0 keeps the local horizon upright; 1 rotates the dial with its view camera. This changes only the instrument display.
- minimumSpeed: number, default 0.05, from 0 to 10, step 0.01. Direction noise floor (m/s): Hide prograde and retrograde direction markers below this speed in the selected reference frame. Does not affect physical velocity.

## Game API used
FLIGHT_HUD_STATE, HudInstrument, HudInstrumentContext, HudInstrumentDefinition, ModuleInitializationV2, ModuleManifest, NAVBALL_HUD, OverlayPrimitive, PresentationSnapshot, PresentationView, RegisteredModule, ServiceKey, Vec

=== Scoped tab transport reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.net-tab]
Same-origin text links and discovery scoped by game and session protocol.
- ID: spacelab.net-tab
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: network
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- net.tab@1.0.0 · TAB_TRANSPORT: ServiceKey<TabNetworkFactory>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: net.tab
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- beatMs: number, default 2000, from 1 to 300000, step 1. Tab heartbeat interval (ms): Heartbeat spacing for same-browser peers when Web Locks are unavailable. No network traffic leaves this browser.
- idleTimeoutMs: number, default 30000, from 1 to 300000, step 1. Tab peer timeout (ms): Wait this long without peer liveness before closing a same-browser connection. Keep it comfortably longer than the heartbeat interval.
- connectTimeoutMs: number, default 5000, from 1 to 300000, step 1. Tab connection timeout (ms): Maximum wait for a hosting tab to answer a new connection request. Independent of established-peer liveness.
- maxTextLength: number, default 67108864, from 1 to 67108864, step 1. Tab message text limit: Maximum UTF-16 characters in an application message and queued backlog. Lowering it can reject large game snapshots or join packages.

## Game API used
Disposable, ModuleInitializationV2, ServiceKey, TAB_TRANSPORT, TabAdvert, TabChannel, TabDiscovery, TabLocks, TabNetwork, TabNetworkFactory, TabNetworkOptions, TextConnection
-> game-api modules.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules]
-> game-api modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
-> game-api platform.ts [api-reference/game-api/platform]
-> game-api tab-transport.ts [api-reference/game-api/tab-transport]

## Guide and example
Example source: examples/modules/net-tab (build with npm run module:build -- examples/modules/net-tab/module.json out.spacemod).

=== Body spin presentation reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.spin-view]
Optional anti-strobe display over pure prescribed sidereal rotation.
- ID: spacelab.spin-view
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline, node
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- view.spin@1.0.0 · SPIN_VIEWS: ServiceKey<SpinViewFactory>

## Requires
- core.rotation@^1.0.0 · CORE_ROTATION: ServiceKey<RotationMath>

## Composition
- Slots: view.spin
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- holdRadiansPerFrame: number, default 0.08726646259971647, from 0.01 to 0.5. Spin hold threshold (rad/frame): Hold a rapidly spinning body visually still above this turn per displayed frame, avoiding strobing during warp. Physics always uses its true angle.
- resumeRatio: number, default 0.6, from 0.1 to 0.9, step 0.05. Spin resume threshold ratio: Resume visible rotation below this fraction of the hold threshold, reducing flicker around the transition.
- releaseSeconds: number, default 0.4, from 0 to 2, step 0.05. Spin release easing (s): Wall seconds to ease a held body back to its true angle. Zero snaps immediately; physical rotation and saves are unchanged.

## Game API used
Body, CORE_ROTATION, ModuleInitializationV2, RotationMath, SPIN_VIEWS, ServiceKey, SpinViewControl, SpinViewFactory, State, Vec, deterministicMath, interpolateState
-> game-api flight-view.ts [api-reference/game-api/flight-view]
-> game-api modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
-> game-api render-math.ts [api-reference/game-api/render-math]
-> game-api rotation.ts [api-reference/game-api/rotation]
-> game-api state.ts [api-reference/game-api/state]

## Guide and example
Example source: examples/modules/spin-view (build with npm run module:build -- examples/modules/spin-view/module.json out.spacemod).

=== Theme and procedural backdrop reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.theme]
Public presentation.themes@1.0.0 service and lifecycle.
- ID: spacelab.theme
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- presentation.themes@1.0.0 · THEMES: ServiceKey<ThemeFactory>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: presentation.themes
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- windowOpacity: number, default 0.96, from 0.4 to 1, step 0.01. Default window opacity: Opacity for viewers without a saved window preference. Saved viewer choices take precedence. Applies to presentation only.
- starDensity: number, default 1, from 0.5 to 2, step 0.05. Default star density: Background star-density multiplier for viewers without a saved choice. These decorative stars have no physical bodies or collision.
- nebulaIntensity: number, default 1, from 0 to 2, step 0.05. Default nebula intensity: Brightness of the procedural nebula background before viewer preferences override it. Does not affect lighting physics.
- gridOpacity: number, default 0.45, from 0 to 1, step 0.01. Default grid opacity: Opacity of the optional background grid before viewer preferences override it. This does not turn the grid on.
- gridSpacing: number, default 80, from 8 to 512, step 1. Default grid spacing (px): Screen-space grid spacing in CSS pixels before viewer preferences override it. Does not change world coordinates or scale.

## Game API used
Camera, Disposable, ModuleInitializationV2, RenderThemeOptions, ServiceKey, THEMES, ThemeBackdropStyle, ThemeController, ThemeCounterStyle, ThemeFactory, ThemeGridStyle, ThemeOptions, ThemePreferences, ThemePreset, ThemeSettings, ThemeWindowStyle

=== Throttle HUD reference [api-reference/module/spacelab.throttle-hud]
A per-seat throttle lever that any game can place in its HUD through the hud.throttle service.
- ID: spacelab.throttle-hud
- Version: 0.1.0
- API: space-sim-game-api/2
- Manifest format: space-sim-module/2
- Kind: provider
- Tier: presentation
- Environments: browser, offline
- Entry: src/index.ts
- Multiplayer: supported

## Provides
- hud.throttle@1.0.0 · THROTTLE_HUD: ServiceKey<HudInstrumentDefinition>

## Requires
Nothing.

## Composition
- Slots: hud.throttle
- Dependencies: none
- Conflicts: none
- Initialize before: none; after: none

## Settings
- keyboardStepPercent: number, default 1, from 0.1 to 100, step 0.1. Arrow-key throttle step (%): Percentage-point change from one Up/Down arrow press on a focused throttle lever. Session ownership and input locks still apply.
- pageStepPercent: number, default 10, from 0.1 to 100, step 0.1. Page-key throttle step (%): Percentage-point change from one Page Up/Page Down press on a focused throttle lever. Home and End still select zero and full thrust.

## Game API used
FLIGHT_HUD_STATE, HudInstrumentDefinition, HudSurface, ModuleInitializationV2, OverlayPrimitive, RegisteredModule, THROTTLE_HUD, ThrottleLeverOptions, ThrottleLeverState

-> game-api presentation.ts [api-reference/game-api/presentation]
-> game-api view-hud.ts [api-reference/game-api/view-hud]

## Guide and example

=== game-api: alarms.ts [api-reference/game-api/alarms]

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/alarms.ts.

## NodeAlarmSettings
Portable reminder preferences; notified survives save/load without saving execution.
    export interface NodeAlarmSettings {
     readonly enabled: boolean; readonly leadSeconds: number; readonly pause: boolean;
     /** Optional local presentation request; absent/false leaves selection and camera alone. */
     readonly focusShip?: boolean;
     readonly notified?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## OrbitalAlarmEvent
Declared orbital reminder event with simulation-second deadline, impulse center time, and pause flag.
    export interface OrbitalAlarmEvent {
     readonly id: string; readonly kind: string; readonly label: string;
     /** Simulation seconds: reminder deadline and physical event/impulse centre. */
     readonly deadline: number; readonly time: number; readonly pause: boolean;
     readonly handled?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## OrbitalAlarmNotice
Delivered orbital alarm notice indicating arrival or missed status when delivered past event time.
    export interface OrbitalAlarmNotice extends OrbitalAlarmEvent { readonly missed: boolean }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## OrbitalAlarmClock
    export interface OrbitalAlarmClock extends Disposable {
     /** Borrowed result buffer, valid until next sync. Each due event fires once, even
      * after a time jump. Session identity or time rewind clears prior in-memory state.
      * Call with the complete current set (max 1024); omitted events are deleted. */
     sync(session: object, now: number, events: readonly OrbitalAlarmEvent[]): readonly OrbitalAlarmNotice[];
     readonly secondsUntilNext: number;
     clear(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## OrbitalAlarmFactory
Factory creating independent simulation-time orbital alarm clocks (`flight.alarms@1.0.0`).
    export interface OrbitalAlarmFactory { create(): OrbitalAlarmClock }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## ORBITAL_ALARMS
Service key for the orbital alarm clock factory (`flight.alarms@1.0.0`).
    export const ORBITAL_ALARMS: ServiceKey<OrbitalAlarmFactory> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'flight.alarms', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## OrbitalAlarmStatus
Read-only inspection; never an arming, delivery or host-control interface.
    export interface OrbitalAlarmStatus extends OrbitalAlarmEvent { readonly now: number; readonly handled: boolean }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-alarms.

## OrbitalAlarmCatalogue
    export interface OrbitalAlarmCatalogue {
     /** Detached snapshot of current events for one opaque session identity, max1024.
      * Clocks own their schedule; removing a clock removes its catalogue entries. */
     read(session: object): readonly OrbitalAlarmStatus[];
    }

=== game-api: context.ts [api-reference/game-api/context]
Declarations from context.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/context.ts.

## SimulationContext
Motion context with an optional numerical/contact replacement and force schedule. All motion is synchronous, body-relative 2D SI: metres, seconds, radians (+Y up). Methods leave input state untouched. Contexts are owned per world/vehicle definition; craft with the same handling share a context. No DOM or timers. Prediction consumers own a clone and dispose it when a job ends or is cancelled.
    export interface SimulationContext {
      readonly modelIdentity: string;
      readonly stepSeconds: 0.01;
      readonly vehicle: Readonly<VehicleDefaults>;
      readonly capabilities: MotionCapabilities;
      readonly services: MotionServices;
      validateState(state: State, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile): void;
      step(state: State, controls: Controls, seconds: number, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile): State;
      advance(state: State, controls: Controls, seconds: number, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile): State;
      predict(start: State, coarse: boolean, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile): Generator<GhostSample, GhostEnd>;
      keplerCoast(state: State, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile, seconds?: number): Coast | undefined;
      coastAt(coast: Coast, time: number, body: Body, surface?: SurfaceProfile, options?: { readonly cache?: boolean }): Propagation;
      coastCacheStats(coast: Coast | undefined): CoastCacheStats;
      /** Same immutable configuration; fresh derived caches and no live mutable state. */
      cloneForPrediction(): SimulationContext;
      /** Release derived caches. Idempotent; the context can rebuild them if reused. */
      dispose(): void;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## SimulationContextFactory
Composition supplies a fresh context for each world, including restores and mirrors.
    export type SimulationContextFactory = (settings: Readonly<VehicleDefaults>, options?: MotionContextOptions) => SimulationContext;
Used by: builtin.motion, spacelab.session-flight.
-> API reference index [api-reference]

=== game-api: engine.ts [api-reference/game-api/engine]

## SavedShip
Body-relative Float64 state. Derived caches and per-step diagnostics are rebuilt.
    export interface SavedShip {
      readonly vehicleId?: string;
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly ownerId: string;
      readonly bodyId: string;
      readonly position: Pair;
      readonly velocity: Pair;
      readonly heading: number;
      readonly throttle: number;
      readonly status: State['status'];
      readonly revision: number;
      readonly godMode?: boolean;
      readonly water?: 'submerged' | 'floating';
      /** Present while coasting: the epoch this ship's exact Kepler motion is evaluated from. */
      readonly coast?: SavedCoast;
      readonly fault?: string;
      readonly moduleState?: Readonly<Record<string, ModuleState>>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## SavedPlayer
A player identity. Only a SHA-256 hash of the secret reconnect token is stored.
    export interface SavedPlayer {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly tokenHash?: string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## WorldRead
Body-relative SI state. Read operations retain the world's established rails semantics.
    export interface WorldRead {
      readonly vehicles?: VehicleCatalog;
      contextFor(ship: Pick<Ship, 'vehicleId'>): SimulationContext;
      readonly data: ModuleDataRead;
      readonly dataAdapters: readonly ModuleDataExtension[];
      readonly configuration?: SavedGameConfiguration;
      /** Absent on older hosts; planners must not assume optional seed arguments are understood. */
      readonly predictionFeatures?: { readonly futureSeeds: boolean; readonly endTime?: boolean };
      readonly id: string; readonly tick: number; readonly time: number;
      readonly systemId: string; readonly size: number; readonly awakeCount: number; readonly asleepCount: number;
      readonly universe: UniverseDefinition; readonly system: LoadedSystem; readonly context: SimulationContext;
      readonly simulationFingerprint: string; readonly lock?: AuthoritativeLock | ModuleLock;
      readonly rules: readonly WorldRuleExtension[]; readonly forces: readonly ForceExtension[]; readonly commands: readonly CommandExtension[];
      readonly moduleState: Readonly<Record<string, WorldRuleState>>;
      readonly soloPreview: boolean; readonly paused: boolean; readonly fault?: string;
      readonly rails: boolean; readonly nextWakeTick: number; readonly requiresWorldPrediction: boolean;
      readonly version: number; readonly nextShipNumber: number; readonly saveVersion?: number;
      readonly migrateState?: (id: string, state: ModuleState, target: number) => ModuleState;
      readonly surfaces: ReadonlyMap<string, SurfaceProfile>;
      record(id: string): Ship | undefined; records(): IterableIterator<Ship>;
      ship(id: string): Ship | undefined; ships(): readonly Ship[]; has(id: string): boolean;
      awakeShips(): IterableIterator<Ship>; previous(id: string): Ship | undefined;
      ownedBy(id: string): ReadonlySet<string>; isAsleep(id: string): boolean; settles(id: string): boolean;
      stateAt(id: string, time: number): State | undefined;
      hasBody(id: string): boolean; body(id: string): ResolvedBody; surface(id: string): SurfaceProfile | undefined;
      presetId(id?: string): string; presetState(id?: string): { readonly state: State; readonly bodyId: string };
      /** Optional inert future seed is supported by independent analytic flight only;
       * rule/custom-frame worlds reject it. Never edits a ship or arms controls. until requests an exact endpoint (at most
       * 100 Julian years after the seed), disabling orbit closure; requires predictionFeatures.endTime. */
      predictShip(id: string, coarse?: boolean, seed?: { readonly state: State; readonly bodyId: string; readonly until?: number }): Generator<GhostSample, GhostEnd>;
      cloneForPrediction(): WorldControl;
    }
Used by: spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## WorldChanges

## WorldControl
    export interface WorldControl extends WorldRead, Disposable {
      readonly data: ModuleDataControl;
      setControls(id: string, controls: Controls): void; setThrottle(id: string, value: number): void;
      step(): WorldEvent[]; advanceTo(tick: number, maxSteps?: number): WorldEvent[];
      put(ship: Ship): void; remove(id: string): boolean;
      spawn(options: { readonly ownerId: string; readonly presetId?: string; readonly name?: string; readonly godMode?: boolean; readonly vehicleId?: string }): Ship;
      respawn(id: string, preset?: string, godMode?: boolean): Ship;
      teleport(id: string, body: string, position: Vec, velocity: Vec, heading?: number): Ship;
      reset(): void; setGodMode(id: string, enabled: boolean): void; setGodModeAll(enabled: boolean): void;
      rename(id: string, name: string): void; clearFault(id: string): void;
      setModuleState(state: Readonly<Record<string, WorldRuleState>>): void;
      adopt(record: Ship, ticks: number, before?: (ship: Ship, tick: number) => Ship): WorldEvent[];
      /** Reconciliation may rewind. No stepping, rounding or epoch conversion occurs here. */
      restoreClock(tick: number, time: number): void;
      /** One owned delta consumer per world, released before another session can subscribe. */
      subscribeChanges(): WorldChanges;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## ControllerProfile
    export interface ControllerProfile {
      /** Axis index for turning [-1..1]. Default: 0 (Left Stick X / Joystick Roll). */
      readonly turnAxis: number;
      readonly invertTurn: boolean;
      /** Axis index for throttle adjustment [-1..1]. Default: 1 (Left Stick Y / Joystick Pitch). */
      readonly throttleAxis: number;
      readonly invertThrottle: boolean;
      /** Axis index for analog zoom, or -1 for none. Default: 3 (Right Stick Y). */
      readonly zoomAxis: number;
      readonly invertZoom: boolean;
      /** Radial deadzone fraction [0..0.5]. Default: 0.15. */
      readonly deadzone: number;
      /** Standard-mapping button indices per action. Held actions (engine, throttle, turn) act
       * while pressed; the rest fire once per press. */
      readonly buttons: Readonly<Record<FlightActionId, readonly number[]>>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-profiles, spacelab.local-pilots.

=== game-api: flight-plans.ts [api-reference/game-api/flight-plans]
Declarations from flight-plans.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/flight-plans.ts.

## MAX_FLIGHT_PLAN_SECONDS
Maximum explicit planning horizon; default display forecasts retain their shorter cap.
    export const MAX_FLIGHT_PLAN_SECONDS = 100 * 365.25 * 86400;
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## flightTimeTolerance
Absolute-time comparisons remain below a fixed tick, including multi-year epochs.
    export const flightTimeTolerance = (time:number):number => …;
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FlightPlanNode
Portable SI intent only. No engine, warp, controls or execution state is persisted. `time` is absolute simulation seconds; `prograde` and `radial` are impulse components in m/s relative to the velocity at the node, in the frame of `bodyId`.
    export interface FlightPlanNode {
     readonly id: string; readonly label: string; readonly bodyId: string;
     readonly time: number; readonly prograde: number; readonly radial: number;
     readonly completed?: boolean;
     readonly alarm?: NodeAlarmSettings;
     /** Use the selected finite-burn controller for progressive evaluation. */
     readonly finite?: boolean;
     /** Optional arrival goal, in SI metres above the destination's reference radius.
      * Intent only: never grants permission to coast, change a node, or ignite. */
     readonly targetOrbit?: { readonly periapsisAltitude:number; readonly apoapsisAltitude:number };
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FlightPlan
One ship's plan, bound to the world and motion model it was captured in. A changed universe, system, simulation fingerprint or motion identity makes every node invalid until the player reviews it. `epoch` is the coast start, local to `bodyId`.
    export interface FlightPlan {
     readonly shipId: string; readonly universeId: string; readonly systemId: string;
     readonly fingerprint: string; readonly motionIdentity: string; readonly bodyId: string;
     readonly epoch: { readonly time: number; readonly position: Vec; readonly velocity: Vec; readonly heading: number };
     readonly nodes: readonly FlightPlanNode[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FlightPlanArchive
    export interface FlightPlanArchive { readonly format: 'space-sim-flight-plans/1'; readonly plans: readonly FlightPlan[] }
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## EvaluatedFlightNode
Per-node evaluation. An invalid node carries a readable `reason`; later nodes are not evaluated past the first failure and repeat its reason.
    export interface EvaluatedFlightNode {
     readonly intent: FlightPlanNode; readonly valid: boolean; readonly reason: string;
     /** Incoming coast seed and state at the burn. All coordinates are local to bodyId. */
     readonly seed?: State; readonly before?: State; readonly after?: State; readonly delta?: Vec;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FlightPlanProgress
    export interface FlightPlanProgress { readonly node: number; readonly sample?: GhostSample }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## FiniteBurnResult
    export interface FiniteBurnResult {readonly after?:State;readonly delta?:Vec;readonly reason:string}
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans.

## FiniteCaptureResult
    export interface FiniteCaptureResult {readonly node?:FlightPlanNode;readonly after?:State;readonly reason:string}
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans.

## FlightPlanService
Bounded inert intent/codec and selected-provider orbital evaluation, without UI or execution.
    export interface FlightPlanService {
     /** Bind the host-selected controller for detached finite evaluation; no execution permission. */
     withBurns?(burns:ManeuverBurnFactory):FlightPlanService;
     /** Detached selected-controller validation; caller owns and cancels the generator. */
     simulateBurn?(world:WorldRead,ship:Ship,node:FlightPlanNode,burns:ManeuverBurnFactory):Generator<void,FiniteBurnResult>;
     refineCapture?(world:WorldRead,ship:Ship,node:FlightPlanNode,burns:ManeuverBurnFactory):Generator<void,FiniteCaptureResult>;
     /** Snapshot the ship's current coast and world identity as a new plan. Throws
      * `Flight plan: ...` for invalid nodes, like `read`. Never changes the world. */
     capture(world: WorldRead, ship: Ship, nodes: readonly FlightPlanNode[]): FlightPlan;
     /** Validate an untrusted `space-sim-flight-plans/1` archive (at most 64 plans of 16 nodes,
      * node times increasing and within 100 Julian years of the epoch). Unknown fields are
      * dropped. Throws an Error whose message starts with `Flight plan:`. */
     read(raw: unknown): FlightPlanArchive;
     /** Fast same-body compatibility evaluation; cross-body legs request progressive evaluation. */
     evaluate(plan: FlightPlan, world: WorldRead): readonly EvaluatedFlightNode[];
     /** Full bounded coast/contact/SOI and optional finite-burn evaluation; yields detached work. */
     evaluateAsync?(plan: FlightPlan, world: WorldRead, traceCoasts?: boolean): Generator<FlightPlanProgress, readonly EvaluatedFlightNode[]>;
     /** Progressive chained preview through the shared world predictor; cancellation closes its child job. */
     predict(plan: FlightPlan, world: WorldRead): Generator<GhostSample, GhostEnd>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FLIGHT_PLANS
Shared flight-plan intent and evaluation, provided by `spacelab.flight-plans` in Space Sim.
    export const FLIGHT_PLANS: ServiceKey<FlightPlanService> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'flight.plans', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## flightSampleInFrame
Translate a timed sample between nonrotating body frames, including velocity. bodyId remains the physical SOI. Encounter snapshots follow the sample frame.
    export function flightSampleInFrame(sample: GhostSample, frameId: string, system: LoadedSystem): GhostSample;
Used by: spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## FlightHost
Authoritative flight session host owning peer connections and world stepping. Borrows the supplied WorldControl; steps the simulation and broadcasts events.
    export interface FlightHost extends Disposable {
      /** Borrowed simulation world control interface. */
      readonly world: WorldControl;
      /** Active host settings. */
      readonly settings: FlightHostSettings;
      /** Host player identifier, if seated. */
      readonly hostId?: string;
      /** Whether the simulation is currently paused. */
      readonly paused: boolean;
      /** Diagnostic reason why the simulation is paused. */
      readonly pauseReason: string;
      /** Active simulation time warp acceleration factor (1x, 2x, etc.). */
      readonly warp: number;
      /** Time warp factor requested by the host or client. */
      readonly requestedWarp: number;
      /** Reason why the requested warp factor was clamped or granted. */
      readonly warpReason: string;
      /** Listener notified whenever players connect, disconnect, or change seats. */
      onplayers: (() => void) | null;
      /** Listener notified when chat messages are posted. */
      onchat: ((line: ChatLine) => void) | null;
      /** Seat the local host player, returning the assigned player ID. */
      seatHost(name: string, token: string, savedHostId?: string): string;
      /** Seat a local player, returning their player ID. */
      seatLocal(name: string, token: string): string;
      /** Unseat a local player by ID. */
      unseatLocal(id: string): void;
      /** Accept an established TextConnection from a remote client. */
      accept(connection: TextConnection): void;
      /** Number of currently connected peer connections. */
      connectedCount(): number;
      /** Number of remote players currently seated. */
      remoteCount(): number;
      /** List active connected session players with names, IDs and ship lists. */
      playerList(): SessionPlayer[];
      /** Snapshot the complete player roster including saved session tokens. */
      roster(): SavedPlayer[];
      /** Retrieve player display name by ID. */
      playerName(id: string): string | undefined;
      /** Set or clear the simulation pause state. */
      setPaused(paused: boolean, reason?: string): void;
      /** Check whether a given player has permission to change time warp. */
      canWarp(playerId?: string): boolean;
      /** Request a change to the simulation warp multiplier. */
      setWarp(factor: number, playerId?: string): { readonly granted: number; readonly reason?: string };
      /** Spawn a new ship in the world for the specified player. */
      spawnShip(playerId: string, presetId?: string, godMode?: boolean, vehicleId?: string): Ship;
      /** Respawn an existing destroyed or lost ship for a player. */
      respawnShip(playerId: string, shipId: string, presetId?: string, godMode?: boolean): Ship;
      /** Remove a ship from the world. */
      removeShip(playerId: string, shipId: string): void;
      /** Set god mode on a specific ship belonging to a player. */
      setGodMode(playerId: string, shipId: string, enabled: boolean): void;
      /** Set god mode on all ships in the world. */
      setGodModeAll(enabled: boolean): void;
      /** Teleport a ship to an orbital body, position, and velocity vector. */
      teleportShip(playerId: string, shipId: string, bodyId: string, position: Vec, velocity: Vec, heading?: number): Ship;
      /** Apply flight control inputs (pitch, yaw, roll, throttle) to a ship. */
      setControls(playerId: string, shipId: string, controls: Controls): void;
      /** Directly set throttle lever position [0, 1] on a ship. */
      setThrottle(playerId: string, shipId: string, throttle: number): void;
      /** Dispatch a named flight command to a ship. */
      command(playerId: string, shipId: string, commandId: string, args?: unknown): void;
      /** Route a custom module data command request. */
      dataCommand(playerId: string, request: ModuleDataCommand): void;
      /** Broadcast a chat message across all connected players. */
      chat(playerId: string | undefined, text: string): void;
      /** Kick a player by ID with an optional reason message. */
      kick(playerId: string, message?: string): boolean;
      /** Advance the world simulation by one discrete tick. */
      step(): WorldEvent[];
      /** Advance the world simulation by a duration in wall seconds. */
      advance(seconds: number): WorldEvent[];
      /** Perform periodic housekeeping (pruning stale connections, heartbeat checks). */
      service(): void;
      /** Close the host session and disconnect all peers with the given reason. */
      close(reason?: string): void;
      /** Generate an authoritative wire snapshot for broadcasting to clients. */
      snapshot(): unknown;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

    export interface FlightCameraController extends Disposable {

       * release it; ordinary pan/zoom retain it. The chosen horizon preference is independent. */
      manualRotation?: number;

## PredictionSummary
Result of one completed tracing pass. `workMs` is wall-clock work, not simulated time; `coarse` is true for a preview traced while the engine was burning.
    export interface PredictionSummary { end: GhostEnd; samples: number; workMs: number; coarse: boolean }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction.

## FlightPrediction
A progressive trajectory preview for one view. It traces on an isolated motion-context clone (or `world.predictShip` when a world is passed) and never advances live authority. Consumers read `path` and `pathEnd`; the remaining fields are diagnostics. `path` samples are body-relative SI (metres, m/s, seconds of simulation time) in the frame each sample names. Arrays are reused between passes: copy a path you need to keep.
    export interface FlightPrediction extends Disposable {
      pass: PredictionPass | undefined; passKey: string; pathKey: string; passCount: number; path: readonly GhostSample[]; pathEnd: GhostEnd;
      lastPass: PredictionSummary;
      /** Wall-clock milliseconds of tracing work allowed per `update`, from 0.1 to 16. */
      maxMs: number;
      /** Cancel the current pass and clear the path; the next `update` starts a new one. */
      invalidate(): void;
      /** Call once per rendered frame. Starts a new pass when the ship, body, surface, motion
       * model or relevant command epoch changed, otherwise continues the current one within
       * `maxMs`. `seconds` is the elapsed wall time used to earn sample work. Throws after dispose. */
      update(context: SimulationContext, state: State, activeBody: ResolvedBody, surface: SurfaceProfile | undefined,
        selectedShipId: string | undefined, seconds: number, engine: boolean, controls?: Controls, world?: WorldRead): void;
      /** Short status for a readout, such as `Full orbit · period 5400.0 s` or `Tracing · 120 points`. */
      text(state: State): string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.maneuver-editor.

=== game-api: join-pack.ts [api-reference/game-api/join-pack]
Declarations from join-pack.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/join-pack.ts.

## JoinPackageRequirement
Metadata description for one module package required by a server join offer.
    export interface JoinPackageRequirement {
      /** Unique module package identifier. */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Semver package version. */
      readonly version: string;
      /** SHA-256 digest of the module package contents. */
      readonly digest: string;
      /** Package file size in bytes. */
      readonly bytes: number;
      /** SPDX license expression or license reference. */
      readonly license: string;
      /** Whether the package contains executable code (requiring user security consent). */
      readonly containsCode: boolean;
      /** Whether source code sidecars are bundled inside the package. */
      readonly sourceIncluded: boolean;
      /** Whether this package is strictly required to join the session. */
      readonly required: boolean;
      /** Architectural tier of the module. */
      readonly tier: 'authoritative' | 'presentation' | 'platform' | 'network';
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-join.

## JoinOffer
Pre-join offer sent by a server listing required module packages and session metadata.
    export interface JoinOffer {
      /** Join offer format schema identifier. */
      readonly format: 'spacelab-join-offer/1';
      /** Unique offer identifier hash. */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Unique server session identifier. */
      readonly sessionId: string;
      /** Human-readable session or world name. */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Session protocol identifier (e.g. `space-sim-flight/1`). */
      readonly protocol: string;
      /** Entity schema identifier (e.g. `space-sim-flight-entities/1`). */
      readonly entitySchema: string;
      /** World physics and universe model identifier. */
      readonly model: string;
      /** Fingerprint identifying the composition of active modules. */
      readonly fingerprint: string;
      /** SHA-256 digest of the lock file. */
      readonly lockDigest: string;
      /** SHA-256 digest of the combined game pack payload. */
      readonly packDigest: string;
      /** Total byte size of the combined game pack payload. */
      readonly packBytes: number;
      /** List of individual module packages included in this game pack. */
      readonly packages: readonly JoinPackageRequirement[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.join-review, spacelab.session-join.

## JoinStage
Isolated staging environment for an incoming game pack. Staged candidate has passed validation; `activate()` commits and publishes once authenticated.
    export interface JoinStage extends Disposable {
      /** Client session join options derived from the staged game environment. */
      readonly options: JoinOptions;
      /** Publish the staged modules into active runtime use upon successful session authentication. */
      activate?(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-join.

## JoinClientPort
Client-side integration port for reviewing, caching, and staging incoming join packs.
    export interface JoinClientPort {
      /** Whether the current client runtime allows loading third-party executable code. */
      readonly allowRuntimeCode: boolean;
      /** Check whether this exact offer has already been approved by the user. */
      approved(offer: JoinOffer): Promise<boolean>;
      /** Prompt the user to review the offer and grant explicit installation consent. */
      review(offer: JoinOffer, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<boolean>;
      /** Check if identical game pack bytes are already available in local cache. */
      cached(offer: JoinOffer): Promise<Uint8Array | undefined>;
      /** Validate and stage the game pack in an isolated temporary environment. */
      stage(offer: JoinOffer, bytes: Uint8Array, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<JoinStage>;
      /** Persist the verified game pack and record scoped user approval. */
      remember(offer: JoinOffer, bytes: Uint8Array, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
      /** Optional callback reporting byte download progress. */
      progress?(received: number, total: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-join.

## JoinNegotiation
Result of a completed prejoin negotiation.
    export interface JoinNegotiation {
      /** Active text connection handed off to the session protocol. */
      readonly peer: TextConnection;
      /** Staged game environment, if custom packages were negotiated. */
      readonly stage?: JoinStage;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-join.

## JoinPackFactory
Pre-join game pack negotiation factory for clients and hosts.
    export interface JoinPackFactory {
      /** Perform client-side prejoin negotiation with the remote peer before session connection. */
      client(peer: TextConnection, port: JoinClientPort, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<JoinNegotiation>;
      /** Host-side prejoin negotiation: delivers offer and chunks, then invokes accept with the authenticated peer. */
      host(peer: TextConnection, source: JoinPackSource, accept: (peer: TextConnection) => void, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
    }

## ModuleManifest
    export interface ModuleManifest {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-module/1';

      readonly authority: 'authoritative' | 'presentation';
      readonly environments: readonly ModuleEnvironment[];
      /** Enabled before this module, which may use their exposed APIs. */
      readonly dependencies: readonly ModuleDependency[];
      /** Modules that cannot be enabled together with this one. */
      readonly conflicts?: readonly ModuleDependency[];
      readonly provides: readonly string[];
      readonly requires: readonly string[];
      readonly slots: readonly string[];
      readonly before: readonly string[];
      readonly after: readonly string[];
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, NumberSetting>>;
      readonly license: string;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.navball.

## ModuleInitialization
Initialization is synchronous in Phase 2. Own resources immediately after allocation. provide() only accepts slots selected for this module by the resolved profile.
    export interface ModuleInitialization {
      /** Resolved values for the manifest's declared settings, after profile overrides and
       * range validation. Read them during initialization; a change prepares a new candidate. */
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
      /** Text assets embedded in an imported code artifact; empty for static modules. */
      readonly assets: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
      /** Own a sequential state migration in this candidate, without global registration. */
      migrateState(fromVersion: number, migrate: (value: ModuleValue) => ModuleValue): void;
      /** Register a resource for disposal with this candidate, in reverse acquisition order.
       * Disposal runs when the candidate is replaced or removed, including after a failed start. */
      own<T extends Disposable>(resource: T): T;
      /** True when the resolved profile selected this module as the provider of `slot`. */
      selected(slot: string): boolean;
      provide(slot: string, service: unknown): void;
      /** Authoritative static modules only; uses the resolved module identity and order. */
      contributeForce(hook: ForceHook): void;
      contributeWorldRule(hook: WorldRuleHook): void;
      /** Authoritative modules only: up to 32 named commands, run by the authoritative host. */
      contributeCommand(hook: CommandHook): void;
      /** Presentation modules only: up to eight of each, in resolved module order. */
      contributeLayer(hook: LayerHook): void;
      contributePanel(hook: PanelHook): void;
      contributeCamera(hook: CameraHook): void;
      /** Publish a read-only API (an object of functions and constants) for dependent modules.
       * At most once. Authoritative state still belongs in module state, never in closures. */
      expose(api: object): void;
      /** The API exposed by one of this module's declared dependencies, which initialize first. */
      dependency<T extends object>(id: string): T;
    }
Used by: spacelab.alarm-panel.

## RegisteredModule
    export interface RegisteredModule {
      readonly manifest: ModuleManifest;
      /** Canonical imported package identity, independent of the execution environment. */
      readonly contentDigest?: string;
      /** Author declaration; numerical/replay conformance remains the author’s responsibility. */
      readonly supportsMultiplayer?: boolean;
      initialize(context: ModuleInitialization): void;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.motion, builtin.navball, spacelab.throttle-hud.

=== game-api: modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]

## ServiceKey
Typed handle for one service slot and exact version, exported by game-api as a constant (for example `THROTTLE_HUD`). Pass it to `provide`, `use` or `tryUse`; never construct a look-alike key for a service you have not declared in the manifest. A phantom function makes service types invariant under strictFunctionTypes.
    export interface ServiceKey<T> {
      readonly slot: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly serviceType?: (value: T) => T;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.motion, builtin.navball, spacelab.alarm-panel, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart, spacelab.chat-console, spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.hud-layout, spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-profiles, spacelab.input-touch, spacelab.local-pilots, spacelab.local-views, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.orbital-alarms, spacelab.player-controller, spacelab.player-view, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer, spacelab.trainer, spacelab.trainer-appearance, spacelab.transfer-planner, spacelab.vector-shaders, spacelab.view-hud.

## ModuleInitializationV2
    export interface ModuleInitializationV2 extends Omit<ModuleInitialization, 'assets' | 'provide'> {
      /** Present on current hosts; older v2 hosts may omit this additive diagnostic identity. */
      readonly module?: ModuleReference;
      readonly assets: ModuleAssets;
      /** Initialization only. Publishes this module's implementation of a declared `provides`
       * entry; throws unless the resolved profile selected this module for the slot. */
      provide<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>, service: T): void;
      /** Initialization only. Returns the bound provider for a declared `requires` entry, which
       * initialized first. Throws for an undeclared, unbound or version-incompatible key.
       * Keep the returned service; it remains valid until this candidate is disposed. */
      use<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>): T;
      /** Initialization only. Absence is allowed only for a declared optional capability
       * with no binding; invalid access and a bound provider's failures still throw. */
      tryUse<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>): T | undefined;
      contributeInput(source: InputSource): void;
      /** Contributes namespaced action definitions to the active candidate (platform tier only). */
      contributeActions(actions: readonly ActionDefinitionV2[]): void;
      contributeTransport(provider: TransportProvider): void;
      contributeDiscovery(provider: DiscoveryProvider): void;
      /** Authoritative records shared by saves and compatible session replication. */
      contributeData(adapter: ModuleDataAdapter): void;
      contributeVehicle(vehicle: VehicleDefinition): void;
      contributeVehicleAppearance(appearance: VehicleAppearance): void;
      contributeWindow(window: WindowDefinition): void;
      /** Bounded structured Manual data, available in every tier without a UI dependency. */
      contributeGuide(page: GuidePage): void;
      contributeMaterial(material: MaterialDefinition): void;
      contributePostEffect(effect: PostEffectDefinition): void;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.motion, builtin.navball, spacelab.alarm-panel, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart, spacelab.chat-console, spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.hud-layout, spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-profiles, spacelab.input-touch, spacelab.join-review, spacelab.local-pilots, spacelab.local-views, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.orbital-alarms, spacelab.player-controller, spacelab.player-view, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer, spacelab.throttle-hud, spacelab.trainer, spacelab.trainer-appearance, spacelab.transfer-planner, spacelab.vector-shaders, spacelab.view-hud.

=== game-api: node-transport.ts [api-reference/game-api/node-transport]
Declarations from node-transport.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/node-transport.ts.

## UpgradeByteStream
Borrowed native byte stream interface (e.g. Node.js `net.Socket`). Protocol providers do not own the HTTP server or port listener.
    export interface UpgradeByteStream {
      /** Number of bytes buffered for writing. */
      readonly writableLength: number;
      /** Whether the stream is open for writes. */
      readonly writable: boolean;
      /** Whether the underlying socket has been destroyed. */
      readonly destroyed: boolean;
      /** Enable or disable TCP_NODELAY (Nagle's algorithm). */
      setNoDelay?(enabled: boolean): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to data chunks received on the socket. */
      on(event: 'data', listener: (data: Uint8Array) => void): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to socket communication errors. */
      on(event: 'error', listener: (error: Error) => void): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to socket closure or termination events. */
      on(event: 'close' | 'end', listener: () => void): unknown;
      /** Write string or byte data to the socket. */
      write(data: string | Uint8Array): unknown;
      /** End the stream, optionally sending final data. */
      end(data?: string | Uint8Array): unknown;
      /** Destroy the stream immediately. */
      destroy(): unknown;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## HttpUpgrade
HTTP upgrade request headers passed from the Node HTTP server.
    export interface HttpUpgrade {
      /** HTTP method (usually 'GET'). */
      readonly method?: string;
      /** Case-insensitive HTTP request headers. */
      readonly headers: Readonly<Record<string, string | readonly string[] | undefined>>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## WebSocketCodecs
Node-only engine primitives; no HTTP/WebSocket policy or connection state.
    export interface WebSocketCodecs {
      /** Compute standard SHA-1 base64 hash for the Sec-WebSocket-Accept handshake. */
      sha1Base64(text: string): string;
      /** Raw DEFLATE with level 1 and sync-flush termination, including its final empty block. */
      deflate(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array;
      /** Raw sync-flushed DEFLATE. Must reject output exceeding maximumBytes. */
      inflate(bytes: Uint8Array, maximumBytes: number): Uint8Array;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## ServerSocketOptions
Configuration options for an accepted server WebSocket connection.
    export interface ServerSocketOptions {
      /** Maximum accepted message size in bytes before closing the connection. */
      readonly maxMessageBytes: number;
      /** Heartbeat ping interval in milliseconds. Zero or negative disables pings. */
      readonly pingIntervalMs: number;
      /** Diagnostic label identifying this server socket or endpoint. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Whether permessage-deflate compression was negotiated. */
      readonly deflate?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

    export interface ServerTextConnection extends TextConnection {

      readonly closed: Promise<void>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

    export interface NodeWebSocketProvider extends Disposable {

       * Accept one borrowed HTTP upgrade or refuse it with an HTTP response.
       * Returns undefined if the handshake headers or key are invalid.
       */
      accept(request: HttpUpgrade, stream: UpgradeByteStream, head: Uint8Array, options: ServerSocketOptions): ServerTextConnection | undefined;
    }

=== game-api: platform.ts [api-reference/game-api/platform]
Declarations from platform.ts used by documented modules.

## InputFrame
Device-local input frame delivered to input sources. `elapsed` is wall-clock seconds (never a simulation step dt). `pilot` is the local seat index (0–3).
    export interface InputFrame {
      /** Wall-clock seconds since the prior frame. Units: seconds. */
      readonly elapsed: number;
      /** Local seat index in [0, 3]. */
      readonly pilot: number;
      /** True when continuous flight control inputs should be sampled. */
      readonly flightAllowed: boolean;
      /** True when global UI and view shortcut actions should be sampled. */
      readonly shortcutsAllowed: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## ActionSink
Synchronous receiver for action values written by an InputSource during sample().
    export interface ActionSink {
      /** Writes an analog or digital action value. Clamped by consumers to [-1, 1]. */
      set(action: string, value: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## ActionDefinitionV2
Namespaced action definition contributed by platform-tier input modules.
    export interface ActionDefinitionV2 {
      /** Namespaced action identifier (e.g. `spacelab.input-actions/engine`). */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Friendly human-readable label shown in control configuration menus. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Action category grouping for UI filtering and suppression. */
      readonly category: 'flight' | 'warp' | 'view' | 'system';
      /** Action trigger mode: continuous hold, bipolar axis, or discrete press. */
      readonly mode: 'held' | 'axis' | 'press';
      /** Default KeyboardEvent.code bindings or modifier chords. */
      readonly defaultKeys: readonly string[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-touch.

## InputSource
Device sampler that translates hardware events or DOM interactions into namespaced actions.
    export interface InputSource extends Disposable {
      /** Human-readable name identifying the hardware source type (e.g. 'keyboard', 'gamepad', 'touch'). */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Synchronous sampling pass writing at most 256 action entries into the sink. */
      sample(frame: InputFrame, into: ActionSink): void;
      /** Releases held inputs for the specified local pilot seat, or all seats if omitted. */
      release(pilot?: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-touch.

## PilotRegistry
Registry managing active local player seats and input device assignments.
    export interface PilotRegistry {
      /** Number of active local player seats in [1, 4]. */
      readonly count: number;
      /** Returns the session player ID for the specified local seat index. */
      playerId(pilot: number): string | undefined;
      /** Assigns an input device ('auto', 'keyboard', 'none', or 'pad:N') to a seat. */
      assign(pilot: number, device: string): void;
      /** Releases held inputs for a seat or all seats. */
      release(pilot?: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.local-pilots.

    export interface LocalPilotHost {

      /** Returns session player ID for a local seat index, if assigned. */
      playerId(pilot: number): string | undefined;
      /** Assigns an input device ('auto', 'keyboard', 'none', or 'pad:N') to a seat index. */
      assign(pilot: number, device: string): void;
      /** Releases held inputs across host-managed controllers. */
      release(pilot?: number): void;
    }

## ControlTargetBinding
Local routing identity connecting a seat and viewport to a session player and target entity.
    export interface ControlTargetBinding {
      /** Local seat identifier. */

      /** Session player identifier. */

## ControlTargetAdapter
Explicit entity or session adapter for routing intent. Implementations validate ownership and execute actions without advancing simulation state. Schema must be 'spacelab-control-target/1'.
    export interface ControlTargetAdapter {
      /** Explicit adapter contract schema identifier. */
      readonly schema: 'spacelab-control-target/1';
      /** Returns true if the session player currently owns and can command the target entity. */
      canControl(binding: ControlTargetBinding): boolean;
      /** Returns the bounded list of namespaced action IDs accepted by this target (max 256). */
      actions(binding: ControlTargetBinding): readonly string[];
      /** Sends an updated action value in [-1, 1] to the controlled entity. */
      setAction(binding: ControlTargetBinding, action: string, value: number): void;
      /** Clears active intent associated with the specified binding. */
      release(binding: ControlTargetBinding): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## PlayerInputBinding
Input source binding for a single local player seat.
    export interface PlayerInputBinding {
      /** Local device seat index in [0, 3]. */
      readonly pilot: number;
      /** Borrowed, ordered input sources (at most 16). Last write wins; provider retains ownership. */
      readonly sources: readonly InputSource[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## PlayerController
Routes namespaced intent from input sources to a compatible control target adapter.
    export interface PlayerController extends Disposable {
      /** Active target binding, or undefined if no target is selected. */
      readonly target: ControlTargetBinding | undefined;
      /** Selects a target entity. Releasing or changing target clears prior intent. */
      select(target: ControlTargetBinding | undefined): void;
      /** Binds input sources for this seat. Reassignment clears held device state. */
      bindInput(input: PlayerInputBinding | undefined): void;
      /** Synchronously samples bound sources and dispatches changed actions to the adapter. */
      sample(frame: Omit<InputFrame, 'pilot'>): void;
      /** Manually sets an action value in [-1, 1] on the active target. */
      set(action: string, value: number): void;
      /** Releases all held actions and informs the adapter. */
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## TextConnection
Bidirectional text transport connection with backpressure and lifecycle events. Shared by browser WebSockets, WebRTC data channels, scoped tab channels, and Node server sockets.
    export interface TextConnection {
      /** Human-readable peer label or endpoint address for diagnostics. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Number of bytes currently queued but not yet transmitted over the wire. */
      readonly bufferedAmount: number;
      /** Transmit an application text message. Dropped or throws if the connection is closed. */
      send(text: string): void;
      /** Close the connection cleanly, optionally passing a reason message to the remote peer. */
      close(reason?: string): void;
      /** Handler invoked when a text message arrives from the remote peer. */
      onmessage: ((text: string) => void) | null;
      /** Handler invoked once when the connection closes, receiving the close reason. */
      onclose: ((reason: string) => void) | null;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join.

## TransportProvider
Pluggable network transport provider capable of opening or listening for reliable text connections.
    export interface TransportProvider {
      /** Transport identifier, such as 'websocket' or 'tab'. */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Supported URL schemes, e.g. ['ws', 'wss'] or ['tab']. */
      readonly schemes: readonly string[];
      /** Signal owns pending IO and the established connection's lifetime. */
      connect(address: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<TextConnection>;
      /** Signal owns pending IO and the listener; accepted peers remain separately owned. */
      listen?(address: string, accept: (peer: TextConnection) => void, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Disposable>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-websocket.

## WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT

    export const WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT: ServiceKey<TransportProvider> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'net.websocket', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.net-websocket.

=== game-api: player-view.ts [api-reference/game-api/player-view]
Declarations from player-view.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/player-view.ts.

## PlayerViewAdapter
One player's adapter instance. Configuration/schema belongs to its provider, allowing future editors without a shared bag of game-specific settings. The view owns this adapter; underlying borrowed services retain their owner.
    export interface PlayerViewAdapter extends Disposable {
      /** Stable underlying resource identity; prevents mutable instances crossing players. */
      readonly resource: object;
      rebind(binding: HudBinding): void;
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerViewAdapters
Map of player view roles to their active adapter instances.
    export type PlayerViewAdapters = Readonly<Partial<Record<PlayerViewRole, PlayerViewAdapter>>>;
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerView
    export interface PlayerView extends Disposable {
      readonly binding: HudBinding;
      readonly adapters: PlayerViewAdapters;
      /** Release all old intent before rebinding. A failed rebind removes every adapter,
       * leaving a neutral view that can be configured again. Seat/view identity is fixed. */
      bind(binding: HudBinding): void;
      /** Replace only this player's parts. Failed preparation retains the old parts,
       * with their intent released; no game world is created or advanced. */
      configure(adapters: PlayerViewAdapters): void;
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerViewFactory
    export interface PlayerViewFactory extends Disposable {
      create(options: { readonly binding: HudBinding; readonly adapters: PlayerViewAdapters }): PlayerView;
      controller(controller: PlayerController, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership,
        target?: (binding: HudBinding) => ControlTargetBinding | undefined): PlayerViewAdapter;
      camera(camera: LocalViewCamera, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership): PlayerViewAdapter;
      hud(hud: ViewHud, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership): PlayerViewAdapter;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

=== game-api: presentation.ts [api-reference/game-api/presentation]

## Camera
System world coordinates (metres, +Y up) to CSS pixels: `scale` pixels per metre, `rotation` radians counter-clockwise applied to the world before projection.
    export interface Camera { readonly center: Vec; readonly rotation: number; readonly scale: number }
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.theme.

## RenderBody
    export interface RenderBody {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly radius: number;
      /** Shared authoritative terrain geometry. */
      readonly surface?: SurfaceProfile;
      readonly color?: string;
      readonly accentColor?: string;
      readonly atmosphereColor?: string;
      readonly horizonColor?: string;
      readonly skyColor?: string;
      readonly surfaceFeatures?: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';
      readonly parentPosition?: Vec;
      readonly orbitRadius?: number;
      /** Drawn angle of the body-fixed frame (terrain, markings) in radians; absent or 0 when it does not spin. */
      readonly rotation?: number;
      /** Unit vector from the body toward its star, in system coordinates. Absent: drawn unlit. */
      readonly sunDirection?: Vec;
      /** Procedural surface look; absent draws a plain disc (low graphics quality). */
      readonly appearance?: BodyAppearance & { readonly colors: readonly string[] };
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RenderShip
Another vessel in the same world: one of this player's fleet, or someone else's.
    export interface RenderShip {
      readonly vehicleId?: string;
      readonly id: string;
      /** System world coordinates. */
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly heading: number;
      readonly status: State['status'];
      readonly engine: boolean;
      readonly throttle: number;
      readonly label: string;
      /** Owned by another player. */
      readonly foreign?: boolean;
      /** Drawn as a small unlabeled rock instead of a vessel (asteroids and debris). */
      readonly rock?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RenderFrame
    export interface RenderFrame {
      readonly vehicleId?: string;
      /** Presentation snapshot; may be interpolated between fixed physics steps. */
      readonly state: State;
      /** Ghost samples with exact velocities. Closed loops are drawn whole; open paths start at the ship. */
      readonly path: readonly GhostSample[];
      readonly pathClosed: boolean;
      readonly camera: Camera;
      readonly engine: boolean;
      /** Celestial bodies in the active system (planets and moons). */
      readonly bodies?: readonly RenderBody[];
      /** Ship position in system world coordinates (defaults to state.position). */
      readonly shipWorldPosition?: Vec;
      /** Reference origin of ghost path (e.g. active body position). */
      readonly pathOrigin?: Vec;
      /** World position of launch pad if any. */
      readonly padPosition?: Vec;
      /** Outward normal at the pad, for its tangent marker. */
      readonly padNormal?: Vec;
      /** Label beside the piloted ship (default VESSEL 01). */
      readonly shipLabel?: string;
      /** Viewer-only annotations; omitted preserves the default visible labels and scale bar. */
      readonly showScale?: boolean;
      readonly showShip?: boolean;
      readonly showPad?: boolean;
      /** Optional explicit viewer animation epoch, seconds. Never advances a simulation. */
      readonly presentationTime?: number;
      readonly showShipLabels?: boolean;
      /** Celestial body names; defaults to visible. Independent of ship labels. */
      readonly showBodyLabels?: boolean;
      /** Planet/moon orbit guides; defaults to visible. Does not hide ship trajectories or affect motion. */
      readonly showBodyOrbits?: boolean;
      /** Other vessels, drawn beneath the piloted ship. */
      readonly ships?: readonly RenderShip[];
      /** Validated presentation layers above the scene, in the overlay section: shapes in order,
       * then text in order so labels stay readable. */
      readonly overlays?: readonly OverlayPrimitive[];
      /** Visual theme settings for universe backdrop, skybox, grids, and timers/counters. */
      readonly theme?: RenderThemeOptions;
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RendererProvider
The `renderer` slot of a presentation profile. A stateless factory: each renderer owns everything it allocates (contexts, programs, buffers, textures) and frees it in dispose(), so a renderer may outlive the candidate whose provider created it.
    export interface RendererProvider {
      readonly id: string;
